"Swap Agreement" means the 1992 Master Agreement (Multicurrency-Cross Border)
published by the International Swaps and Derivatives Association, Inc. ("ISDA") and the
Schedule thereto entered into on the Signing Date between the Issuer, the Swap Counterparty
and the Representative of the Noteholders, as supplemented by the confirmation documenting

                                          102
"Swap Assignee" means any third party to which, following the Signing Date and pursuant to
the Swap Agreement, the Swap Counterparty may assign all or part of its interest in any
amount payble by the Issuer to the Swap Counterparty;

"Swap Counterparty" means Deutsche Bank AG, London Branch and includes any
successor swap counterparty appointed from time to time under the Swap Agreement;

"Swap Counterparty Nominal Amounts" means the nominal amounts paid under the Swap
Transaction by the Swap Counterparty to the Issuer (identified in the Swap Transaction as
"Party A Nominal Amounts") prior to an early termination of the Swap Transaction;

"Swap Issuer Nominal Amounts" means the nominal amounts effectively received under the
Swap Transaction by the Swap Counterparty (identified in the Swap Transaction as "Party B
Nominal Amounts") prior to an early termination of the Swap Transaction;

"Swap Outstanding Nominal Amount" means the Swap Issuer Nominal Amounts less the
Swap Counterparty Nominal Amounts, provided that if the resulting amount is a negative
number such amount will be deemed equal to zero;

"Swap Transaction" means the interest rate swap transaction entered into between the Issuer
and the Swap Counterparty on the Signing Date documented under the Swap Agreement;

"Swap Trigger" means the early termination of the Swap Transaction due to either:

(a)    (i) the downgrade, below one or more of the Minimum Swap Counterparty Ratings,
       of the unsecured and unsubordinated debt obligations of the Swap Counterparty, and
       (ii) the failure by the Swap Counterparty to take such action as is required in the
       Swap Agreement to remedy such downgrade; or

(b)    the occurrence of an Event of Default (as defined in the Swap Agreement (which, for
       the avoidance of doubt, is not the same as an Event of Default under the Notes) and
       designated as such by the Issuer) in respect of which the Swap Counterparty is the
       defaulting party; or

(c)    the occurrence of a Tax Event or an Illegality (both as defined in the Swap
       Agreement) with Party A designating an Early Termination Date for the Swap
       Transaction.

"Swap Unpaid Assigned Amount" means any amount payable by the Issuer under the Swap
Agreement assigned by the Swap Counterparty to the Swap Assignee pursuant to the Swap
Agreement and not paid by the Issuer prior or on the Early Termination Date (as defined in
the Swap Agreement) of the Swap Transaction;

"TARGET" means the Trans-European Automated Real-Time Gross Transfer System (or any
successor thereto);

"TARGET Settlement Day" means any day on which TARGET is open;

      "Transaction Accounts" means the Collection Account, the Expenses Reserve Account, the

      "Transaction Bank" means Deutsche Bank S.p.A. in its capacity as transaction bank, under

      "Transaction Documents" means the English Law Transaction Documents together with the

2.    Form, denomination and title

(a)   Form
      The Notes are in bearer and dematerialised form and will be wholly and exclusively deposited
      with Monte Titoli in accordance with article 28 of Italian legislative decree No. 213 of 24
      June 1998, through the authorised institutions listed in article 30 of such legislative decree.

(b)   Denomination

      The Notes are issued in the denomination of €50,000.

(c)   Title
      The Notes will be held by Monte Titoli on behalf of the Noteholders until redemption and
      cancellation for the account of each relevant Monte Titoli Account Holder. The Notes will at
      all times be in book entry form and title to the Notes will be evidenced by book entries in
      accordance with the provisions of: (i) article 28 of Italian legislative decree No. 213 of 24
      June 1998; and (ii) resolution No. 11768 of 23 December 1998 of the CONSOB as
      subsequently amended. No physical document of title will be issued in respect of the Notes.

(d)   Holder Absolute Owner


                                                104
      The Issuer, the Representative of the Noteholders and any Paying Agent may (to the fullest
      extent permitted by applicable laws) deem and treat the Monte Titoli Account Holder as the
      absolute owner for all purposes (whether or not the Note shall be overdue and
      notwithstanding any notice of ownership or writing on the Note or any notice of previous loss
      or theft of the Note).

3.    Status, ranking and priority

(a)   Status
      The Notes are limited-recourse obligations of the Issuer and, accordingly, the extent of the
      obligation of the Issuer to make payments under the Notes is limited to the share of the
      amounts received or recovered by or on behalf of the Issuer or the Representative of the
      Noteholders in respect of the Claims and under the Transaction Documents. The Notes are
      secured over certain assets of the Issuer pursuant to the Italian Deed of Pledge and the
      English Deed of Charge and Assignment. The Noteholders acknowledge that the limited-
      recourse nature of the Notes produces the effects of a contratto aleatorio under Italian law
      and they accept the consequences thereof, including but not limited to the provisions of
      article 1469 of the Italian civil code. The rights arising from the Italian Deed of Pledge and
      the English Deed of Charge and Assignment are included in each Note.

(b)   Ranking

      In respect of the obligations of the Issuer to pay interest and repay principal on the Notes, the
      Notes rank at all times pari passu without any preference or priority among themselves.

(c)   Sole obligations
      The Notes are obligations solely of the Issuer and are not obligations of, or guaranteed by,
      any other parties to the Transaction Documents.

(d)   Pre-Enforcement Priority of Payments

      Prior to the service of an Issuer Acceleration Notice, the Issuer Available Funds as calculated
      on each Calculation Date will be applied by the Issuer on the Interest Payment Date
      immediately following such Calculation Date in making payments or provisions in the
      following order of priority (the "Pre-Enforcement Priority of Payments") but, in each case,
      only if and to the extent that payments or provisions of a higher priority have been made in
      full:
      (i)      first, in or towards satisfaction, pro rata and pari passu, of any and all outstanding
               fees, costs and expenses of and all other amounts due and payable to the
               Representative of the Noteholders or any appointee thereof;
      (ii)     second, in or towards satisfaction, pro rata and pari passu, of any and all outstanding
               taxes due and payable by the Issuer (to the extent not paid by the Financing Party
               under the Letter of Undertaking);
      (iii)    third, in or towards satisfaction, pro rata and pari passu, according to the respective
               amounts thereof, of:
               (A)       any and all outstanding fees, costs, liabilities and any other expenses to be
                         paid in order to preserve the corporate existence of the Issuer, to maintain it
                         in good standing, to comply with applicable legislation and to fulfill


                                                  105
                obligations to third parties (not being Other Issuer Creditors) incurred in the
                course of the Issuer's business (to the extent not paid by the Financing Party
                under the Letter of Undertaking); and
        (B)     any and all outstanding fees, costs, expenses and taxes required to be paid in
                connection with the listing, deposit or credit ratings of the Notes, or any
                notice to be given to the Noteholders or the other parties to the Transaction
                Documents;
(iv)    fourth, in or towards satisfaction, pro rata and pari passu, according to the respective
        amounts thereof, of any and all outstanding fees, costs and expenses of and all other
        amounts due and payable to the Paying Agents, the Agent Bank, the Computation
        Agent, the Corporate Services Provider, the Stichting Corporate Services Provider,
        the Auditors, the Transaction Bank and the Servicer, each under the Transaction
        Documents to which it is a party;
(v)     fifth, provided that a Swap Trigger has not occurred, in or towards satisfaction, pro
        rata and pari passu, of any and all Swap Unpaid Assigned Amounts (if any) payable
        to the Swap Assignee under the terms of the Swap Agreement following the early
        termination of the Swap Transaction;
(vi)    sixth, in or towards satisfaction, pro rata and pari passu, of any and all amounts due
        and payable by the Issuer to the Swap Counterparty under the Swap Agreement,
        including any termination payment due and payable by the Issuer under the Swap
        Agreement other than any termination payment due and payable by the Issuer to the
        Swap Counterparty upon the occurrence of a Swap Trigger;
(vii)   seventh, in or towards payment, pro rata and pari passu, of all amounts of interest
        due and payable on the Notes;
(viii) eighth, on the Decree 239 Interest Payment Date and on each Interest Payment Date
       thereafter, in or towards repayment, pro rata and pari passu, of principal on the Notes
       in an amount equal to the applicable Scheduled Repayment Amount and the
       Scheduled Repayment Amount Arrears (if any);
(ix)    ninth, in or towards satisfaction of any and all Swap Unpaid Assigned Amounts (if
        any) payable to the Swap Assignee under the terms of the Swap Agreement,
        following the occurrence of a Swap Trigger;
(x)     tenth, in or towards satisfaction of any termination payment due and payable to the
        Swap Counterparty under the terms of the Swap Agreement following the occurrence
        of a Swap Trigger (and, for the avoidance of doubt, not already paid to the Swap
        Counterparty pursuant to item (v) above or to the Swap Assignee pursuant to item (ix)
        above);
(xi)    eleventh, if and to the extent that the Notes have been repaid in full, in or towards
        satisfaction of all amounts of interest due and payable to the Subordinated Loan
        Provider under the Subordinated Loan Agreement;
(xii)   twelfth, if and to the extent that the Notes have been repaid in full, in or towards
        satisfaction of all amounts of principal due and payable to the Subordinated Loan
        Provider under the Subordinated Loan Agreement;
(xiii) thirteenth, if and to the extent that the Notes have been repaid in full, in or towards
       satisfaction of all amounts due and payable to DB under the terms of the Subscription
       Agreement;


                                          106
       (xiv)   fourteenth, if and to the extent that the Notes have been repaid in full, in or towards
               satisfaction, pro rata and pari passu, of all amounts due and payable to the Seller, in
               respect of the Seller's Claims (if any) under the terms of the Transfer Agreement and
               the Transfer Deeds;
       (xv)    fifteenth, if and to the extent that the Notes have been repaid in full, in or towards
               satisfaction, pro rata and pari passu, according to the respective amounts thereof, of
               all amounts due and payable to the Seller:
               (a)     in connection with a limited recourse loan under the Letter of Undertaking;
               (b)     under the terms of the Warranty and Indemnity Agreement; and
       (xvi)   sixteenth, to credit the remainder (if any) to the Collection Account.

(e)   Post-Enforcement Priority of Payments

      At any time following delivery of an Issuer Acceleration Notice, or, in the event that the Issuer
      opts for the early redemption of the Notes under Condition 7(c) (Optional redemption in whole
      for taxation, legal or regulatory reasons), all amounts received or recovered by or on behalf of
      the Issuer or the Representative of the Noteholders in respect of the Claims, the Note Security
      and/or any of the other Transaction Documents (but, for the avoidance of doubt, excluding (A)
      if a Swap Trigger has not occurred the amount standing to the credit of the Collateral Account
      (if any) which exceeds the termination amount (if any) that would have otherwise been
      payable by the Swap Counterparty to the Issuer had the collateral not been provided; and (B) if
      a Swap Trigger has occurred the amount standing to the credit of the Collateral Account (if
      any) which exceeds the greater of (x) the termination amount (if any) that would have
      otherwise been payable by the Swap Counterparty to the Issuer had the collateral not been
      provided, and (y) the Swap Outstanding Nominal Amount) will be applied by or on behalf of
      the Representative of the Noteholders in the following order (the "Post-Enforcement Priority
      of Payments") but, in each case, only if and to the extent that payments of a higher priority
      have been made in full:
      (i)      first, in or towards satisfaction, pro rata and pari passu, of any and all outstanding
               fees, costs and expenses of and all other amounts due and payable to the
               Representative of the Noteholders or any appointee thereof;
      (ii)     second, in or towards satisfaction, pro rata and pari passu, of any and all outstanding
               taxes due and payable by the Issuer (to the extent not paid by the Financing Party
               under the Letter of Undertaking);
      (iii)    third, in or towards satisfaction, pro rata and pari passu, according to the respective
               amounts thereof, of:
               (A)      any and all outstanding fees, costs, liabilities and any other expenses to be
                        paid in order to preserve the corporate existence of the Issuer, to maintain it
                        in good standing, to comply with applicable legislation and to fulfill
                        obligations to third parties (not being Other Issuer Creditors) incurred in the
                        course of the Issuer's business (to the extent not paid by the Financing Party
                        under the Letter of Undertaking); and
               (B)      any and all outstanding fees, costs, expenses and taxes required to be paid in
                        connection with the listing, deposit or credit ratings of the Notes, or any
                        notice to be given to the Noteholders or the other parties to the Transaction
                        Documents;

(iv)     fourth, in or towards satisfaction, pro rata and pari passu, according to the respective
         amounts thereof, of any and all outstanding fees, costs and expenses of and all other

         Agent, the Corporate Services Provider, the Stichting Corporate Services Provider,

(v)      fifth, provided that a Swap Trigger has not occurred, in or towards satisfaction, pro

(viii)   eighth, in or towards repayment, pro rata and pari passu, of the Principal Amount

         due and payable to the Subordinated Loan Provider under the Subordinated Loan
         Agreement;
(xii)    twelfth, in or towards satisfaction, pro rata and pari passu, of all amounts of principal
         due and payable to the Subordinated Loan Provider under the Subordinated Loan
         Agreement;
(xiii)   thirteenth, in or towards satisfaction of all amounts due and payable to DB under the
         terms of the Subscription Agreement; and
(xiv)    fourteenth, in or towards satisfaction, pro rata and pari passu, according to the
         respective amounts thereof, of all amounts due and payable to DB:
